### 91. assistant (2026-07-08T06:04:14.731Z)

# L4-primary pipeline topology — collapse the pipeline onto the worker, shrink the control-plane to a thin router

> **Status:** `DRAFT` — **owner-review** (recommends *against* the epic's proposed direction #1; see §0/§8) · **Owner:** Avi · **Created:** 2026-07-08 · **Last updated:** 2026-07-08
> **Lifecycle:** `DRAFT → IN PROGRESS → DONE → archived` (move to `docs/archives/` when DONE)
> **Issue:** [#524](https://github.com/Khelsutra/badminton-highlight-indexer/issues/524) (epic) · **Builds on:** [#521](https://github.com/Khelsutra/badminton-highlight-indexer/issues/521) (`phase_placement`) · **Motivated by:** [#520](https://github.com/Khelsutra/badminton-highlight-indexer/issues/520) (the tracing that surfaced the data-movement finding)
> **Relation to existing docs:** extends [COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING/README.md](./README.md) (ADR-014) and [PHASE_PLACEMENT.md](./PHASE_PLACEMENT.md) (#521); it does **not** supersede them — it answers the *topology* question they left open and sequences the migration on top of `phase_placement`.
> **Honesty note:** each claim is tagged `[verified]` (checked against code/runlog), `[researched]` (needs sign-off / external pricing I could not fetch live), or `[design]` (proposed). Not legal/financial advice.

---

## 0. TL;DR

The 2026-07-08 live CUJ moved one 1.83 GiB clip **3×** (upload→bucket, bucket→control-plane to validate, bucket→worker to detect) and pinned the CPU-only 2-vCPU control-plane for **~16 min** of heavy work (validation 183 s + stitch ~12m55s). The epic proposes fixing this by **uploading directly onto the L4** and async-copying to the bucket. **This design recommends *against* that direction** and quantifies why: a same-region bucket↔compute transfer inside GCP is **LAN-fast and not billed as internet egress** `[researched]`, so the round-trip that upload-onto-L4 saves is worth **~seconds and ~$0**, while keeping an L4 up to *receive* a **minutes-long** upload burns **~175 s of idle GPU per run (~$0.08–0.16)** `[design]` and *opens* a durability exposure window instead of closing one.

The cheaper, more durable, already-ratified path is the opposite: **keep the upload streaming to the cheap bucket** (the #480 design — the durable copy exists *before* the L4 ever starts), and **collapse validate + detect + stitch onto the scale-to-zero Cloud Run L4 Job** — which is exactly what **#521's `phase_placement` already wires as a config flip**. The residual "~30 s pre-validation gap" is then killed not by moving the *upload* but by the control-plane **never downloading the file at all** (it delegates validation to the L4 per #521, and trusts the already-known submit-time md5 per this epic's win #2 — no re-hash). Net data movement drops from 3× to the theoretical floor: **1 client upload + 1 LAN-fast intra-region pull**, with **zero idle-GPU cost** and **zero exposure window**.

**Actionable now (this PR):** the design doc + **win #2** (drop the redundant control-plane re-hash, config-guarded, reversible). **Win #1** (validation-on-L4) is **delivered by #521** — this doc does not duplicate its knob. A persistent GPU (GCE VM / Cloud Run Service-with-GPU) is **rejected at current volume** on a quantified cost basis and left as an owner-gated future ADR.

## 1. Problem & goal

**Why now.** Tracing the live CUJ (`runlogs/DEPLOY_REPORT_cloud-serving_validation-latency_2026-07-07.md` §9 `[verified]`) produced two findings:

1. **Data is moved 3× per run** `[verified]`: (a) upload streams straight to `gs://…/uploads/…` (never touches control-plane disk — the #480/#471 OOM fix, `backend/api/uploads.py`), computing the md5 incrementally as it streams; (b) the control-plane re-downloads the whole clip to scratch to validate — `_execute_processing` → `storage.acquire_local_input(gs://…)` at `backend/orchestration.py:748`, then a **redundant re-hash** `compute_video_id` at `:751`; (c) the worker downloads it a third time to detect (`[worker] pulled …`). The unlogged **~30 s pre-validation gap** is finding (b).
2. **The control-plane is the scaling wall** `[verified]`: validation (183 s) **and** stitch (~12m55s, ~67 s/clip 4K re-encode, no NVENC) both run in-process on the 2-vCPU control-plane, serialized by `_LOCAL_COMPUTE_LANE` → ~16 min of heavy CPU per run → the API saturates past ~5 concurrent users.

**The concrete outcome we want.** The L4 worker becomes the primary compute for the whole pipeline; the control-plane shrinks to a **thin router** (auth · dispatch · DB · SPA + job status · signed-URL download) that does **no heavy decode/encode** and, ideally, **never pulls the full file**. Every move must be **reversible by config** with a **bucket-mediated fallback**, and land as small PRs on top of `origin/master`.

**The hard question this doc must resolve (compute topology).** "Upload directly to the L4" needs an **inbound endpoint on the GPU box**, but the current L4 is a Cloud Run **Job** (batch, no inbound HTTP — `backend/compute/cloud_run.py`, dispatched per-request, `[verified]`). So the epic forces a choice between **A. Cloud Run Service-with-GPU**, **B. GCE L4 VM**, and **C. a hybrid** presign/redirect. §4 answers it with numbers.

**Read to get current:** [PHASE_PLACEMENT.md](./PHASE_PLACEMENT.md) (#521 — the `phase_placement` knob this sequences on), [README.md](./README.md) §2 decisions **D4/D7/D16** (the ratified constraints that bound the options), and the runlog §9 (the evidence).

## 2. Decisions locked

| # | Decision | Source / date | Implication |
|---|----------|---------------|-------------|
| L1 | **Keep upload → bucket** (streaming, #480). The durable copy exists **before** any compute runs; the bucket *is* the "reliable co-located disk" the epic wants — no VM needed for durability. | this doc `[design]` + #480 `[verified]` | No exposure window; upload cost stays on the cheap I/O path. |
| L2 | **Collapse validate + detect + stitch onto the scale-to-zero Cloud Run L4 Job** via `phase_placement` (#521), not onto a persistent GPU. Honors **D7** (scale-to-zero) + **D16(a)** (Jobs, not Services). | #521 `[verified]` + D7/D16 | The "L4-primary" vision is realized by **config flips**, not a rearchitecture. |
| L3 | **Kill the ~30 s gap by *not downloading to the control-plane*, not by uploading onto the L4.** Compose #521's validation-delegation (CP skips the validator suite) with win #2 (CP trusts the submit-time md5 → no re-hash). | this doc `[design]` | The CP stops pulling the full file when validation is delegated; movement → 3×→2×. |
| L4 | **Reject persistent-GPU (GCE VM / Service-with-GPU) at current volume.** Breakeven vs scale-to-zero is a **~51 % on-demand / ~31 % spot duty cycle** (≈ 2,600–4,300 runs/mo sustained) `[design]`; we are at pilot volume. Revisit only behind a **new ADR** at sustained load. | §4.2 cost model `[design]` | Preserves per-run billing (D7/D8); avoids ~$382–637/mo idle. |
| L5 | **Reject "upload onto the L4."** It saves a ~free, seconds-fast intra-region LAN pull at the cost of ~175 s idle GPU/run **and** opens a durability window. | §4.3 `[design]` | The epic's proposed direction #1 is not adopted; owner sign-off requested (§8). |
| L6 | **Win #2 (drop the re-hash) is config-guarded + reversible** (`deployment.trust_submit_video_id`, default on) and produces a **byte-identical `video_id`** (the GCS `md5Hash` over the same bytes = `compute_video_id`). | win #2 `[verified]` | Zero-regression; one-flag rollback. |

*(IDs are `L#` to avoid colliding with README.md's `D#`. These refine — never override — D4/D7/D16.)*

## 3. Foundation — the ratified constraints this must live inside

The topology is **not a blank slate** — ADR-014 (README.md §2) already ratified the serving substrate. The options in §4 are scored **against** these, and any option that breaks one needs a new ADR, not just this doc:

| Ratified decision | What it pins | Consequence for #524 |
|---|---|---|
| **D4** | In cloud-serving, **stitch runs on Cloud Run** (co-located, metered). | The live system's CP-stitch is a *deviation* from D4; #521 *realizes* D4. #524 is mostly **completing an already-ratified decision**, not inventing one. |
| **D7** | **Scale-to-zero, no warm pool** (a ~$500/mo idle GPU "would break per-video billing"). | Any persistent-GPU option (B, Service-with-min-instances) **contradicts D7** → new-ADR territory (§4.2). |
| **D16(a)** | **Cloud Run Jobs** (not Services-with-GPU), **bucket-poll** for completion. | Option A (Service-with-GPU inbound upload) **contradicts D16(a)**. The Job model is load-bearing (reuses `JobWaiting`/`claim_due_job`). |
| **D16(b)** | Stitch encode = libx264 for parity; **NVENC deferred behind a knob**. | #521 lands exactly that knob (`cloud_stitch_encoder`), using this CUJ as the cost data D16(b) said to revisit on. |
| **D16(c)** | Input cap hard-reject `< 2 GB`. | The 1.83 GiB CUJ clip is *at* the cap; the topology must stay sane at 2 GB (upload minutes, pull seconds). |

**Key reframing this foundation gives us:** the owner's "reliable persistent disk" requirement is *already met* by the **regional GCS bucket** — a durable, co-located, cheap "disk" the upload already writes to directly. The persistent-VM instinct solves a problem (durable local disk) that the bucket-first design doesn't have.

## 4. Design / architecture — resolving the topology question

### 4.1 The measured pipeline (evidence base) `[verified]`

From runlog §9 (rev `rally-control-plane-00019`, "First CUJ.MP4", 1.83 GiB / 174.17 s / 4K-class):

| Phase | Wall | Machine | GPU needed? | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Upload (client → bucket) | **~2m55s** | client uplink | no | 1.83 GiB @ ~84 Mbps — **owner bandwidth**, not infra |
| Accept → validate start | ~30 s | control-plane | no | **the gap** = CP re-download to scratch (`acquire_local_input` + re-hash) |
| Validation (#512 one-pass) | **183 s** | control-plane 2 vCPU | no (CPU-bound) | scene_cut/blur/relevance shared decode |
| Dispatch → worker ready | ~55 s | Cloud Run Job | — | image pull + GCSFuse + torch/cuda init (cold start) |
| Detection (WASB) | **161.6 s** | L4 (cuda) | **yes** | the only genuinely GPU-bound phase |
| Ingest + finalize | ~4 s | control-plane | no | intervals → DB |
| Compile / stitch | **~12m55s** | control-plane 2 vCPU | no (NVENC would need GPU) | 11× 4K re-encode+watermark |

**Two facts that drive the whole design:**
- **Only *detection* is GPU-bound.** Validation is CPU/decode-bound; stitch is encode-bound (NVENC *wants* a GPU but libx264 doesn't *need* one). So "collapse onto the L4" is about **co-locating CPU work next to the GPU on a box that's already up and already holding the bytes**, not about the GPU per se.
- **Upload dwarfs compute** (175 s upload vs 161.6 s GPU-detect). Anything that keeps a GPU allocated *during upload* roughly **doubles** the GPU-allocated time for zero compute.

### 4.2 Cost model (asia-southeast1, USD, Cloud Billing Catalog API, 2026-07-08) `[verified]` rates / `[design]` per-run

Rates pulled live from the Catalog API (`cloudbilling.googleapis.com`, project `gen-lang-client-0306026826`):

| Resource | Rate | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Cloud Run **L4 GPU** (no zonal redundancy) | **$0.00022404 / s** = $0.806/hr | Catalog `[verified]` |
| Cloud Run vCPU (instance/jobs) | $0.0000216 / s | Catalog `[verified]` |
| Cloud Run memory | $0.0000024 / GiB·s | Catalog `[verified]` |
| → **L4 Job @ 8 vCPU / 32 GiB (running)** | **≈ $1.705 / hr** ($0.000474/s) | derived `[design]` |
| GCE **g2-standard-4** (4 vCPU/16 GiB + L4), on-demand | **$0.872 / hr** = **~$637 / mo** | Catalog `[verified]` |
| GCE g2-standard-4, **spot/preemptible** | $0.523 / hr = ~$382 / mo | Catalog `[verified]` |
| Cloud Run **GPU Service, warm** (min-instances=1, 4 vCPU/16 GiB) | ≈ $1.256 / hr = **~$917 / mo** idle | derived `[design]` |
| Balanced PD | $0.11 / GiB·mo | Catalog `[verified]` |

**Per-run GPU-allocated cost (the term that varies by topology):**

| Topology | GPU-allocated time / run | GPU cost / run | Δ vs today | Concurrency wall? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Today** (detect-only on L4; validate+stitch on CP) | ~222 s (cold+detect+io) | **~$0.105** | — | **Yes** — ~16 min CP CPU/run |
| **#521 + win #2** (validate+detect+stitch on L4; upload→bucket) | ~312 s (validate 60 + detect 162 + stitch 60 + io 30) | **~$0.148** | **+$0.04** | **No** — CP is thin |
| **Upload-onto-L4** (GPU up during upload too) | ~487 s (+175 s idle upload) | **~$0.231** | **+$0.13** | No, but idle-GPU tax |
| **Persistent GCE VM** (on-demand) | billed 24/7 regardless | **$637/mo flat** | breaks per-run billing | No |

**Breakeven (persistent vs scale-to-zero):** a persistent on-demand g2 ($0.872/hr) beats the scale-to-zero Job ($1.705/hr *while running*) only at a **duty cycle ≥ $0.872/$1.705 ≈ 51 %** (spot: ≈ 31 %). At ~312 s/run that is **~2,600–4,300 runs/month sustained, 24/7** `[design]`. Pilot volume is *handfuls/day*. **Scale-to-zero wins by ~2 orders of magnitude** and preserves per-run billing (D7/D8). A persistent GPU is also **idle >95 % of the time** at our volume — the ~$382–637/mo would be almost pure waste.

### 4.3 The three topology options, scored

**Option A — Cloud Run Service-with-GPU hosting upload + pipeline.**
- *Latency:* scale-to-zero → same ~55 s cold start as the Job; **min-instances=1** removes cold start but pins a **~$917/mo warm idle GPU**.
- *Cost:* to accept the upload, the GPU Service instance must be **up for the whole 175 s upload** → the idle-GPU tax of §4.2 on *every* request, plus the warm-idle bill if min-instances>0.
- *Ops/parity:* **contradicts D16(a)** (Jobs, not Services) and **D7** (scale-to-zero) — abandons the `JobWaiting`/`claim_due_job`/bucket-poll machinery that's shipped and tested. A Service also needs request-timeout/concurrency tuning the batch Job sidesteps.
- **Verdict: reject.** Buys an inbound endpoint we don't need (the bucket is the inbound endpoint) at the cost of the idle-GPU tax + two ratified-decision reversals.

**Option B — GCE L4 VM (persistent, reliable PD).**
- *Reliability:* real, attached, reliable PD — the owner's stated appeal. But **the bucket already gives us durable co-located storage** (L1), so the PD solves a non-problem.
- *Cost:* **$382–637/mo flat**, >95 % idle at pilot volume; breaks per-run billing (D7/D8). Spot is cheaper but **preemptible** — directly against the "reliable" motivation.
- *Ops:* we now **own a VM** — patching, disk-fill, crash-recovery, autoscaling-group if we ever need >1. The Job model has none of this.
- **Verdict: reject at current volume; owner-gated future ADR** if sustained load crosses the §4.2 breakeven.

**Option C — Hybrid: control-plane presigns/redirects the upload to a just-spun-up L4, pipeline runs there, async copy to bucket.**
- *The fatal timing problem:* compute needs the **whole** file, so the L4 cannot start useful work until the upload finishes. Spinning the L4 up *to receive* the upload means the GPU is **allocated and idle for the entire 175 s upload** — the §4.2 idle tax, same as A. Upload and compute **cannot overlap**.
- *Durability:* introduces an **exposure window** (upload-complete → async-mirror-confirmed) where the only copy is on L4 local disk; a preemption/crash in that window **loses the source** unless the client re-uploads (see §4.5).
- **Verdict: reject.** It pays the idle-GPU tax *and* takes on a durability window, to save a transfer that is ~free and seconds-fast.

**Option A′ (recommended) — co-located Jobs, bucket-mediated upload (the L1–L3 design).**
- Keep **upload → bucket** (cheap, bounded-memory, durable-immediately). Keep the **scale-to-zero L4 Job**. Move **validate + stitch** onto the Job via `phase_placement` (#521). Make the CP **stop downloading** (delegate validation + trust the md5, win #2). The Job pulls the file **once** from the same-region bucket at LAN speed.
- *Data movement:* **1 client upload + 1 intra-region LAN pull** — the floor. (Detection and validation/stitch all run in **one** Job execution, so it's one pull, not two.)
- *Cost:* +$0.04/run GPU vs today, **−~16 min CP CPU/run** (the actual scaling win). No idle tax, no warm bill, no VM.
- *Durability:* **no exposure window** — the bucket copy exists before the Job starts.
- *Ops/parity:* **honors D4/D7/D16**; reuses the shipped dispatch/poll path; every phase is a **config flip with an in-process fallback** (#521's `_dispatch_*_remote` already logs-and-falls-back to in-process when the box isn't cloud-wired).

> **The one-line intuition:** the bucket *is* the co-located reliable disk; the GPU should attach only for the ~5 min of compute, pulling from it at LAN speed — never sit idle for the ~3 min upload.

### 4.4 The minimal control-plane surface (the "thin router")

After A′, the control-plane does **only** I/O-light, decode-free work — trivially horizontally scalable (the one caveat is SQLite; a Postgres move is out of scope, noted in §6):

| Keep on the control-plane | Why it must stay | Code anchor `[verified]` |
|---|---|---|
| **Cloudflare-Access edge auth** (verify `Cf-Access` JWT) | D9 — one identity system; direct-hit spoofing guard | `backend/api/auth.py`, gated at `/api/process` |
| **Upload endpoint** (streams parts → bucket) | Bounded-memory, no decode (#480); the durable-copy producer | `backend/api/uploads.py` |
| **Dispatch / routing** (submit job, dispatch Cloud Run Job, bucket-poll) | Owns the async job lifecycle (`JobWaiting`/`claim_due_job`) | `backend/orchestration.py`, `backend/compute/cloud_run.py` |
| **DB writes** (jobs, videos, `play_segments`, validation verdict) | The control-plane owns the SQLite DB (worker uses a throwaway scratch DB) | `backend/infrastructure/database*.py` |
| **SPA serving + job status** (`/api/jobs/{id}` polling) | The UX surface | `backend/api/jobs.py`, `backend/ui/` |
| **Signed-URL download** (`/api/highlights` 307) | Reel is already in the bucket; CP just signs | `backend/main.py::get_highlights` |
| **~~Validation~~** | → **L4** via `phase_placement.validation` (#521) | delegated |
| **~~Stitch/compile~~** | → **L4** via `phase_placement.compile` (#521) | delegated |
| **~~Full-file download + re-hash~~** | → **eliminated** (delegate validation + trust md5, win #2) | this PR |

### 4.5 The async copy-to-bucket durability model (spec — needed *only* if a future upload-onto-L4 path is ever adopted)

The recommended A′ design **has no async-copy step** (the bucket copy is the *upload*, produced before compute). This section specs the model the epic asked for, so that if the owner overrides §8 and adopts upload-onto-L4, the durability semantics are pinned rather than improvised:

- **When the mirror runs:** kick a background `storage.put(local → gs://…/uploads/…)` **immediately on upload-complete** (not "during segmentation" — starting it later only lengthens the exposure window; there's no compute reason to delay it). Run it concurrently with validation/detect.
- **Failure/resume:** retry with capped exponential backoff; the mirror is **idempotent** (same object key = the md5). On worker preemption before the mirror confirms, the run is **lost** — the job must fail with a *retryable* status so the client can re-submit (the source is still on the client). **Never** report success until the mirror is **confirmed** (object exists + size/md5 match).
- **Exposure window:** upload-complete → mirror-confirmed, the **only** durable copy is L4 local PD. Window length ≈ mirror wall (1.83 GiB intra-region ≈ seconds–tens of seconds `[design]`). A preemption in-window = re-upload. **This window is the entire reason A′ is preferred** — A′'s window is *zero*.
- **Ack contract:** the client keeps the source until the API returns `mirror_confirmed:true`; only then may it release it. (A′ needs no such contract — bucket-first.)

## 5. Phased migration — every step a config flip with a bucket-mediated fallback

Built on #521's `resolve_phase_placement(cfg, phase)` so **placement is config, not code**. Each phase is independently reversible (`control_plane` ⇄ `cloud_run_l4`) and falls back to in-process on a non-cloud box.

| Phase | Change | Mechanism | Fallback | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **P0** | *Today* — detect on L4; validate+stitch on CP | `compute_target=cloud_run` | in-process | shipped `[verified]` |
| **P1** | **Stitch → L4** | `phase_placement.compile=cloud_run_l4` (+ `cloud_stitch_encoder`) | in-process stitch | **#521 (open)** |
| **P2a** | **Drop the CP re-hash** (trust submit-time md5) | `deployment.trust_submit_video_id` (default on) | re-hash on the drain | **this PR (win #2)** |
| **P2b** | **Validation → L4** (CP stops running validators) | `phase_placement.validation=cloud_run_l4` (guards: requires cloud segment) | CP validates | **#521 (open)** |
| **P2 net** | **CP never downloads the file** → the ~30 s gap dies; movement 3×→2× | P2a ∧ P2b together | either half alone degrades gracefully | needs #521 **+** win #2 merged |
| **P3** | Worker serializes its **validation verdict** back (per-validator results + #518 cache warm), so a worker-side reject renders the same SPA detail as a CP reject | new `report.json`/marker field + CP ingest | worker reject = rc≠0 + message (today) | **follow-up** (see §6) |
| **P4** | *If and only if* sustained load crosses the §4.2 breakeven | persistent worker (VM/Service) | scale-to-zero Job | **owner-gated, new ADR** |

**The "collapse onto the L4" the epic asks for = P1 + P2, both config flips on #521's knob + this PR's win #2.** No rearchitecture, no new infra, fully reversible.

## 6. Honest limits — what this does NOT do / does NOT prove

- **A green suite does not prove the cloud path** — the tests are fully-mocked/offline (a fake `CloudRunJobClient`). P2's "CP never downloads" and the idle-GPU/round-trip numbers are validated **in the cost model + code paths**, not on live infra. A live CUJ on rev ≥ `00020` (owner-side, D17 budget) is required to *confirm* the gap is gone and the movement is 2×.
- **Network-egress "free intra-region" is `[researched]`, not live-verified** — general web egress is blocked here, so same-region GCS↔Cloud Run being un-billed-as-internet-egress rests on documented GCP behavior, not a fetched invoice. If it were *not* free, A′ is *still* cheapest (it moves the fewest bytes); the conclusion is robust to this uncertainty.
- **Win #2 changes nothing when no trusted id is present** — local/appliance uploads and non-GCS direct-URI submits (`submit_time_video_id` → `None`) still re-hash. The win applies to the GCS cloud-serving path (the CUJ case).
- **P3 (worker validation-verdict serialization) is a real gap #521 leaves open** `[verified]`: today a worker-side validation reject surfaces as `rc≠0` + a message, but the per-validator `ValidationResult` list (`result['results']` the SPA renders) and the #518 `validation_cache` warm are **not** reconstructed on the CP. Until P3, moving validation to the L4 (P2b) trades richer verdict UX + cache-warming for the CPU offload. Called out so P2b isn't flipped on blind.
- **SQLite is the residual CP scaling limit** — the thin router is stateless *except* the SQLite DB; true horizontal scale-out needs Postgres (out of scope; noted).
- **The #513 asymmetry reverses under P2b** `[verified]`: worker-side validators run at model-default thresholds unless the CP forwards `validation.*` as `--set`. #521's validation-delegation path must forward those (or re-inherit the divergence that caused the #513 incident). Flagged for the #521 review, not owned here.

## 7. Deliverables & progress tracker ⟵ source of truth

Legend: ☐ Todo · ◐ In progress · ☑ Done · ⛔ Gated. One small PR per row, branched from `origin/master`, no stacking.

| ID | Deliverable | Depends on | Gated? | Status | PR |
|----|-------------|-----------|--------|--------|----|
| P0 | **This design doc** (resolves topology A/B/C; recommends A′; specs durability + phased migration) | — | owner-review | ☑ (this PR) | #524 |
| P1 | **Win #2** — drop the CP re-hash; trust submit-time md5; `deployment.trust_submit_video_id` (default on), reversible | — | No | ☑ (this PR) | #524 |
| P2 | **Win #1** — validation-on-L4 (`phase_placement.validation`) | #521 | No | ☑ **delivered by #521** — not duplicated here | #521 |
| P3 | **CP-download-avoidance measured live** — confirm the ~30 s gap is gone + movement is 2× on rev ≥ `00020` | #521 + P1 merged | ⛔ owner-side (live spend, D17) | ☐ | — |
| P4 | **Worker validation-verdict serialization** (§6 gap) so P2b keeps rich SPA verdict + #518 cache | #521 | No | ☐ follow-up | — |
| P5 | **Persistent-GPU re-evaluation** only if load crosses §4.2 breakeven | volume | ⛔ new ADR | ☐ | — |

## 8. Open questions — owner / external

**Blocking (owner sign-off):**
1. **Do you accept rejecting "upload directly onto the L4" (L5)?** The epic proposed it; this doc recommends against it with the §4.2/§4.3 idle-GPU-vs-free-LAN-pull numbers. A′ delivers the same "collapse onto the L4" outcome, cheaper and more durable, via config. *If you still want upload-onto-L4* (e.g. for a future no-bucket edge deployment), §4.5 specs its durability model — but it needs a Service-with-GPU or VM (a D16(a)/D7 reversal → new ADR).
2. **Flip P2b (validation→L4) before or after P3 (verdict serialization)?** P2b alone offloads CPU but degrades the reject-UX + skips #518 cache-warm (§6). Recommend: land P3 first, then P2b.

**Nice-to-know:**
3. Is pilot volume expected to cross the §4.2 breakeven within the planning horizon? (Decides whether P5 is ever live.)

## 9. Definition of done

- [x] Design doc resolves the topology question (A/B/C scored) with a clear recommendation (A′) + quantified cost/latency/reliability. `[this PR]`
- [x] Minimal control-plane surface defined (§4.4). `[this PR]`
- [x] Async copy-to-bucket durability model specified with failure/resume + exposure window (§4.5). `[this PR]`
- [x] Phased migration laid out on #521's `phase_placement`, each step a config flip with a bucket-mediated fallback (§5). `[this PR]`
- [x] Win #2 landed (config-guarded, reversible, tests green). `[this PR]`
- [ ] Win #1 confirmed delivered by #521 (not duplicated). `[cross-PR]`
- [ ] Live CUJ confirms the gap is gone + movement is 2× (P3, owner-side). `[gated]`
- [ ] Owner signs off on L5 (reject upload-onto-L4) → flip Status → IN PROGRESS/DONE.

**Internal code anchors `[verified]`:** `backend/api/uploads.py` (streamed md5), `backend/orchestration.py:220-239` (`submit_time_video_id`, #484) / `:741-763` (re-hash) / `_maybe_dispatch_remote`, `backend/api/jobs.py:80-153` (submit-time cache already trusts the md5), `backend/api/job_worker.py:122-193` (`_job_to_request`/`_run_claimed_job`), `backend/compute/cloud_run.py` (dispatch + bucket-poll), `backend/config/placement.py` (#521 `resolve_phase_placement`), `backend/worker/__main__.py` (worker validation + `--skip-validation`).

### 94. user (2026-07-08T06:04:27.872Z)

1	"""Markdown internal-link checker — fail CI on a broken internal link in a LIVE doc.
2	
3	Scans tracked ``*.md`` files (via ``git ls-files``) and, for each ``[text](dest)`` / ``![alt](dest)``
4	link whose destination is INTERNAL (a repo path and/or a ``#anchor``), verifies that
5	
6	  - the target file exists (resolved relative to the linking file, or the repo root for ``/abs``), and
7	  - any ``#anchor`` matches a GitHub-style heading slug in the target markdown file.
8	
9	SKIPPED: external links (``http(s)``/``mailto``/``tel``/``ftp``/protocol-relative ``//``), links inside
10	fenced code blocks, and any file under ``docs/archives/**`` — archived docs are frozen snapshots whose
11	relative links are historical-by-design (see ``docs/DOC_STATUS.md`` §2). Mirrors ``tools/leak_scan.py``:
12	run ``python -m tools.check_md_links`` (exit 1 on any broken internal link in a live doc).
13	"""
14	
15	from __future__ import annotations
16	
17	import argparse
18	import re
19	import subprocess
20	import sys
21	from pathlib import Path
22	from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Sequence, Set, Tuple
23	
24	#: Source docs under these prefixes are NOT scanned — archived = frozen snapshots whose relative
25	#: links are historical-by-design (DOC_STATUS.md §2 step 5).
26	EXCLUDE_PREFIXES: Tuple[str, ...] = ("docs/archives/",)
27	#: This tool + its test legitimately embed sample/broken links — never scan them.
28	SELF_EXCLUDE: Tuple[str, ...] = ("tools/check_md_links.py", "tests/test_check_md_links.py")
29	
30	_LINK_RE = re.compile(r"!?\[[^\]]*\]\(\s*([^)]+?)\s*\)")
31	_HEADING_RE = re.compile(r"^(#{1,6})\s+(.*?)\s*#*\s*$")
32	_FENCE_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*(?:```|~~~)")
33	#: a scheme (``http:``, ``mailto:``, …) or protocol-relative ``//`` ⇒ external, not our concern.
34	_EXTERNAL_RE = re.compile(r"^(?:[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9+.\-]*:|//)")
35	
36	
37	def slugify(heading: str) -> str:
38	    """GitHub-style heading → anchor slug (matches `github-slugger`): lowercase, drop punctuation
39	    (keep word chars / spaces / hyphens), then replace EACH space with a hyphen — crucially NOT
40	    collapsing, so a heading with removed punctuation (e.g. 'a / b' → 'a  b') yields '--' like GitHub."""
41	    s = re.sub(r"[^\w\s-]", "", heading.strip().lower())
42	    return s.replace(" ", "-")
43	
44	
45	def heading_slugs(text: str) -> Set[str]:
46	    """The set of GitHub anchor slugs for a markdown file's headings (with -1/-2 dedup suffixes)."""
47	    counts: Dict[str, int] = {}
48	    out: Set[str] = set()
49	    in_fence = False
50	    for line in text.splitlines():
51	        if _FENCE_RE.match(line):
52	            in_fence = not in_fence
53	            continue
54	        if in_fence:
55	            continue
56	        m = _HEADING_RE.match(line)
57	        if not m:
58	            continue
59	        base = slugify(m.group(2))
60	        n = counts.get(base, 0)
61	        out.add(base if n == 0 else f"{base}-{n}")
62	        counts[base] = n + 1
63	    return out
64	
65	
66	def _split_dest(dest: str) -> Tuple[str, str]:
67	    """``path#anchor`` (with an optional ``"title"`` suffix) → ``(path, anchor)``."""
68	    dest = dest.strip().split()[0]  # drop a trailing "title"
69	    path, _, anchor = dest.partition("#")
70	    return path, anchor
71	
72	
73	def check_file(repo: Path, rel: str, slug_cache: Dict[str, Set[str]]) -> List[Tuple[int, str]]:
74	    md = repo / rel
75	    base = md.parent
76	    findings: List[Tuple[int, str]] = []
77	    in_fence = False
78	    for i, line in enumerate(md.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines(), 1):
79	        if _FENCE_RE.match(line):
80	            in_fence = not in_fence
81	            continue
82	        if in_fence:
83	            continue
84	        for m in _LINK_RE.finditer(line):
85	            dest = m.group(1).strip()
86	            if not dest or _EXTERNAL_RE.match(dest):
87	                continue
88	            path, anchor = _split_dest(dest)
89	            if path == "":
90	                target = md  # pure same-file anchor
91	            elif path.startswith("/"):
92	                target = repo / path.lstrip("/")
93	            else:
94	                target = (base / path).resolve()
95	            if path != "" and not target.exists():
96	                findings.append((i, f"broken link -> {dest} (no file {path})"))
97	                continue
98	            if anchor and target.suffix.lower() == ".md" and target.is_file():
99	                key = str(target)
100	                if key not in slug_cache:
101	                    slug_cache[key] = heading_slugs(
102	                        target.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
103	                    )
104	                if slugify(anchor) not in slug_cache[key]:
105	                    findings.append((i, f"broken anchor -> {dest} (no heading #{anchor})"))
106	    return findings
107	
108	
109	def tracked_md(repo: Path) -> List[str]:
110	    out = subprocess.run(
111	        ["git", "ls-files", "*.md"], cwd=str(repo), capture_output=True, text=True
112	    )
113	    files = sorted({f.strip() for f in out.stdout.splitlines() if f.strip()})
114	    return [
115	        f for f in files if not f.startswith(EXCLUDE_PREFIXES) and f not in SELF_EXCLUDE
116	    ]
117	
118	
119	def scan_repo(repo: Path) -> List[Tuple[str, int, str]]:
120	    slug_cache: Dict[str, Set[str]] = {}
121	    results: List[Tuple[str, int, str]] = []
122	    for rel in tracked_md(repo):
123	        try:
124	            for lineno, reason in check_file(repo, rel, slug_cache):
125	                results.append((rel, lineno, reason))
126	        except OSError:
127	            continue
128	    return results
129	
130	
131	def main(argv: Optional[Sequence[str]] = None) -> int:
132	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(
133	        description="Check internal markdown links in LIVE docs (excludes docs/archives/)."
134	    )
135	    ap.add_argument(
136	        "--root",
137	        type=Path,
138	        default=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1],
139	        help="repo root (default: the parent of tools/)",
140	    )
141	    args = ap.parse_args(list(argv) if argv is not None else None)
142	    results = scan_repo(args.root)
143	    if not results:
144	        print("[check-md-links] clean — no broken internal links in live docs.")
145	        return 0
146	    print(f"[check-md-links] {len(results)} broken internal link(s):", file=sys.stderr)
147	    for rel, lineno, reason in results:
148	        print(f"  {rel}:{lineno}: {reason}", file=sys.stderr)
149	    return 1
150	
151	
152	if __name__ == "__main__":
153	    raise SystemExit(main())
154

457	
458	    **Pure / additive:** the empty default profile (``""``) applies NO preset, so the pipeline

460	    ``deployment.profile`` or the ``DEPLOYMENT_PROFILE`` env var); auto-detect only ever
461	    *suggests* one (D15 — cloud spend is never entered silently). Nothing in the core reads
462	    these fields yet (M1): selecting a profile works purely by pre-setting the EXISTING knobs
463	    (``indexing.detector_impl`` / ``skip_ai_handoff``, ``storage.backend``) in the loader.
464	
465	    Valid values mirror ``backend/config/presets.py`` (parity-pinned by a unit test)."""
466	
467	    # "" = no profile (today's manual knobs). Others apply presets.PROFILE_PRESETS.
468	    profile: Literal["", "truly-local", "cloud-serving", "cpu-mock"] = ""
469	    # Where the core runs. Set by the profile preset; overridable in config.json. Inert in M1

471	    compute_target: Literal["", "local_gpu", "cloud_run", "cpu_mock"] = ""

473	    # bucket-poll CONCURRENTLY when compute_target=="cloud_run". Each is an I/O-bound Cloud Run Job
474	    # execution (the GPU work is on ephemeral L4s, WASB-only has no Gemini), so — unlike a truly-local
475	    # single box — they need not serialize. Bounds concurrent PAID L4 executions. Non-cloud_run targets
476	    # ignore this and stay serial (max_workers=1), preserving the truly-local GPU/Gemini serialization.
477	    max_concurrent_remote_jobs: int = Field(default=4, ge=1, le=64)
478	    # Poll budget for one dispatched remote job (CONTROL_PLANE_DURABLE_REBUILD §4.3). Must sit
479	    # ABOVE the worker Job's own --task-timeout (3600s) so the worker's timeout stays the
480	    # authority and the dispatcher's one-shot post-timeout marker rescue still applies — the old
481	    # 1800s ExecutionPolicy default undercut real 1080p60 jobs (~22 min detect + queue/cold-start,
482	    # observed on the 2026-07-05 owner CUJ) and could abandon a slow-but-healthy paid GPU run.
483	    remote_poll_max_wait_sec: float = Field(default=3900.0, gt=0)

487	    """Per-job cost ledger (COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING M8, D8).
488

492	    version is the seeded ``placeholder-v0`` (every rate ``0.0`` → cost ``0``) until the owner

24	def _md5_hex(md5_b64: "str | None") -> Optional[str]:
25	    """GCS reports ``md5Hash`` as base64 of the raw digest; ``compute_video_id`` (the DB key)
26	    is a HEX digest — convert so the two are directly comparable (#484). Composite objects
27	    have no md5Hash → None; a malformed value is treated as absent, never raised."""
28	    if not md5_b64:
29	        return None
30	    try:
31	        raw = base64.b64decode(md5_b64, validate=True)
32	    except (binascii.Error, ValueError):
33	        return None
34	    return raw.hex() if len(raw) == 16 else None
35

37	class GCSStorage(StorageBackend):
38	    def __init__(
39	        self,
40	        *,
41	        client: Any = None,
42	        project: Optional[str] = None,
43	        signed_url_ttl: int = 3600,
44	        **_ignored,
45	    ) -> None:
46	        self._ttl = signed_url_ttl
47	        if client is not None:
48	            self._client = client
49	            return
50	        storage_module: Any = import_module("google.cloud.storage")
51	        self._client = storage_module.Client(project=project)
52	
53	    def _blob(self, ref: StorageRef):
54	        return self._client.bucket(ref.bucket).blob(ref.key)
55	
56	    def fetch_to_local(self, ref, dst=None, *, overwrite=False) -> str:
57	        if dst is None:
58	            raise ValueError("GCSStorage.fetch_to_local requires a dst path")
59	        import os
60	
61	        dst = str(dst)
62	        if os.path.exists(dst) and not overwrite:
63	            return dst

1	"""Validation-result cache keyed by video_id (task 4585aa00).
2	
3	``video_id`` is the MD5 of the file bytes (backend/utils/hashing.py), so an identical-content
4	REUPLOAD keys the same cache row. When that content was already validated under the SAME validator
5	configuration, ``_execute_processing`` reuses the stored verdict instead of re-running the
6	(minutes-long on a heavy 4K clip) validator suite. This is distinct from the pre-existing
7	whole-pipeline cache, which only short-circuits on a prior SUCCESSFUL segmentation — so a reupload
8	after a SEGMENTATION FAILURE used to re-pay the full validation from scratch (the 2026-07-07 CUJ).
9	
10	These tests drive ``_execute_processing`` with a stubbed validator suite (a spy that COUNTS how many
11	times it actually runs) + a stubbed segmenter, so the assertions are on validation-run count, not on
12	real decoding. Cardinal guarantees exercised: same content + same config → validators run ONCE across
13	two uploads; a validation-config change forces a re-run; ``force=true`` bypasses; a cached FAIL is
14	replayed without a re-decode; a different content never reuses another video's verdict.
15	"""
16	
17	import logging
18	
19	import backend.main as m
20	from backend.config.models import AppConfig
21	from backend.infrastructure.database import Database
22	from backend.pipeline.validators.base import ValidationResult, registry
23	from backend.results import Failure
24	
25	_ORCH_LOGGER = "backend.orchestration"
26	
27	
28	def _pass():
29	    return [ValidationResult(validator_name="x", passed=True, message="ok", details={})]
30	
31	
32	def _fail():
33	    return [
34	        ValidationResult(
35	            validator_name="blur_check",
36	            passed=False,
37	            message="too blurry",
38	            details={},
39	        )
40	    ]
41	
42	
43	class _ValSpy:
44	    """Stands in for ``registry.run_all_with_context`` and counts real invocations.
45	
46	    A cache HIT must NOT call this — ``calls`` staying flat across a second upload is the proof that
47	    validation was skipped (the whole point of the cache)."""
48	
49	    def __init__(self, results):
50	        self._results = results
51	        self.calls = 0
52	
53	    def __call__(self, *a, **k):
54	        self.calls += 1
55	        return list(self._results), {}
56	
57	
58	class _FailSeg:
59	    """A segmenter whose detection FAILS (Failure result) — reproduces "validation passed, detection
60	    failed → no cached segments", the exact state the whole-pipeline cache can't short-circuit."""
61	
62	    def __init__(self, db_, cfg_):
63	        pass
64	
65	    def process_video(self, *a, **k):
66	        return Failure("detector crashed")
67	
68	
69	class _OkSeg:
70	    """A segmenter that writes one play row (never reached when validation fails first)."""
71	
72	    def __init__(self, db_, cfg_):
73	        self.db = db_
74	
75	    def process_video(self, vid, path, *a, **k):
76	        sid = self.db.add_play_segment(vid, 0.0, 5.0)
77	        return [
78	            {
79	                "id": sid,
80	                "start_time": 0.0,
81	                "end_time": 5.0,
82	                "duration": 5.0,
83	                "metadata": {},
84	            }
85	        ]
86

88	def _setup(tmp_path, monkeypatch, *, results, seg, cfg=None, content=b"identical-bytes"):
89	    """Wire the module globals + a real (temp-file) DB + stubbed validator/segmenter, and return
90	    ``(db, spy, video_path)``. The video file's BYTES fix its video_id, so writing the same bytes
91	    twice (a 'reupload') hits the same cache key."""
92	    db = Database(str(tmp_path / "t.db"))
93	    monkeypatch.setattr(m, "db_instance", db)
94	    monkeypatch.setattr(m, "global_config", cfg or AppConfig())
95	    spy = _ValSpy(results)
96	    monkeypatch.setattr(m.registry, "run_all_with_context", spy)
97	    monkeypatch.setattr(m.segmenter_registry, "get", lambda name: seg)
98	    video = tmp_path / "v.mp4"
99	    video.write_bytes(content)
100	    return db, spy, str(video)

103	def _process(video, **kw):

107	# --- the CUJ: reupload after a segmentation failure must NOT re-validate --------------------------

110	def test_reupload_after_segmentation_failure_skips_validation(tmp_path, monkeypatch, caplog):

### 135. assistant (2026-07-08T06:10:11.822Z)

"""#524 win #2 — trust the submit-time ``video_id`` instead of re-hashing on the drain.

The chunked upload streams the whole-file MD5 while it uploads (``backend/api/uploads.py``), and the
submit hook records that same md5 on the job row (``submit_time_video_id``, #484 — one gs:// metadata
stat, converted to the identical HEX form ``compute_video_id`` produces). So when the drain fetches
the clip to scratch it already KNOWS the video_id and need not re-hash the (often multi-GB) file.

These tests pin the behaviour: with a well-formed trusted id and the knob on (default), the drain
SKIPS ``compute_video_id`` and uses the trusted id verbatim; with the id absent, malformed, or the
knob off, it hashes exactly as before. A spy on ``compute_video_id`` (counting invocations) is the
proof — mirrors ``tests/test_validation_cache.py``'s stubbed-suite pattern (fully offline, no decode).
"""

import backend.main as m
from backend.config.models import AppConfig, DeploymentConfig
from backend.infrastructure.database import Database
from backend.orchestration import _looks_like_video_id
from backend.pipeline.validators.base import ValidationResult

_VALID_ID = "a" * 32  # a well-formed 32-char lowercase-hex video_id
_HASH_SENTINEL = "b" * 32  # what a real compute_video_id() would return (spied)


class _HashSpy:
    """Stands in for ``compute_video_id`` and counts real invocations. A TRUST hit must NOT call this
    — ``calls == 0`` is the proof the drain skipped the re-hash."""

    def __init__(self, ret=_HASH_SENTINEL):
        self._ret = ret
        self.calls = 0

    def __call__(self, *a, **k):
        self.calls += 1
        return self._ret


class _OkSeg:
    """A segmenter that writes one play row (validation is stubbed to pass, so it's reached)."""

    def __init__(self, db_, cfg_):
        self.db = db_

    def process_video(self, vid, path, *a, **k):
        sid = self.db.add_play_segment(vid, 0.0, 5.0)
        return [
            {"id": sid, "start_time": 0.0, "end_time": 5.0, "duration": 5.0, "metadata": {}}
        ]


def _pass_validators(*a, **k):
    return [ValidationResult(validator_name="x", passed=True, message="ok", details={})], {}


def _setup(tmp_path, monkeypatch, *, cfg=None):
    """Wire module globals + a temp-file DB + a hash spy + stubbed validators/segmenter.
    Returns ``(hash_spy, video_path)``. A bare/local ``video_path`` makes ``acquire_local_input`` an
    identity (no fetch), so the ONLY video_id source is the trusted id or the spied hash."""
    db = Database(str(tmp_path / "t.db"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(m, "db_instance", db)
    monkeypatch.setattr(m, "global_config", cfg or AppConfig())
    spy = _HashSpy()
    monkeypatch.setattr(m, "compute_video_id", spy)
    monkeypatch.setattr(m.registry, "run_all_with_context", _pass_validators)
    monkeypatch.setattr(m.segmenter_registry, "get", lambda name: _OkSeg)
    video = tmp_path / "v.mp4"
    video.write_bytes(b"some-bytes")
    return spy, str(video)


def _process(video, *, trusted, cfg=None):
    return m._execute_processing(
        m.ProcessRequest(video_path=video, segmenter="motion"),
        trusted_video_id=trusted,
    )


# --- _looks_like_video_id (the format guard) -------------------------------------------------------


def test_looks_like_video_id_accepts_only_32_char_lowercase_hex():
    assert _looks_like_video_id("a" * 32)
    assert _looks_like_video_id("0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef")
    # rejects: wrong length, uppercase, non-hex, non-str, empty, None
    assert not _looks_like_video_id("a" * 31)
    assert not _looks_like_video_id("a" * 33)
    assert not _looks_like_video_id("A" * 32)  # helper is strict-lowercase (callers .lower() first)
    assert not _looks_like_video_id("g" * 32)
    assert not _looks_like_video_id("not-a-hash")
    assert not _looks_like_video_id("")
    assert not _looks_like_video_id(None)
    assert not _looks_like_video_id(12345678901234567890123456789012)  # int, not str


# --- the drain: trust vs re-hash -------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_trusts_valid_submit_id_and_skips_rehash(tmp_path, monkeypatch, caplog):
    """A well-formed trusted id + the default knob → the drain reuses it and does NOT call the hash."""
    spy, video = _setup(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    with caplog.at_level("INFO", logger="backend.orchestration"):
        out = _process(video, trusted=_VALID_ID)
    assert out["video_id"] == _VALID_ID
    assert spy.calls == 0  # the whole point: no re-hash
    assert any("trusting submit-time video_id" in r.message for r in caplog.records)


def test_hashes_when_no_trusted_id(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """No trusted id (local upload / non-gcs URI → submit_time_video_id was None) → hash as before."""
    spy, video = _setup(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    out = _process(video, trusted=None)
    assert out["video_id"] == _HASH_SENTINEL
    assert spy.calls == 1


def test_ignores_trusted_id_when_knob_off(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """deployment.trust_submit_video_id=False forces the re-hash even with a valid trusted id
    (belt-and-suspenders rollback, no redeploy)."""
    cfg = AppConfig(deployment=DeploymentConfig(trust_submit_video_id=False))
    spy, video = _setup(tmp_path, monkeypatch, cfg=cfg)
    out = _process(video, trusted=_VALID_ID, cfg=cfg)
    assert out["video_id"] == _HASH_SENTINEL
    assert spy.calls == 1


def test_ignores_malformed_trusted_id_and_falls_back_to_hash(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """A malformed / wrong-length / wrong-encoding trusted id can never become the DB key —
    it degrades to a real hash."""
    spy, video = _setup(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    for bad in ("not-a-valid-id", "a" * 31, "A" * 32, ""):
        spy.calls = 0
        out = _process(video, trusted=bad)
        assert out["video_id"] == _HASH_SENTINEL, bad
        assert spy.calls == 1, bad


def test_uppercase_hex_trusted_id_is_normalized_then_trusted(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """The drain lower-cases before the guard, so an upper-case-hex id (a defensive edge) is
    normalized and trusted — it is still an authoritative md5, just cased differently."""
    spy, video = _setup(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    out = _process(video, trusted="A" * 32)
    assert out["video_id"] == _VALID_ID  # normalized to lowercase
    assert spy.calls == 0


# --- the wiring: the drain hands _execute_processing the job row's video_id -------------------------


def test_run_claimed_job_threads_job_video_id_as_trusted(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """``_run_claimed_job`` must pass the job row's persisted video_id (submit_time_video_id, #484) to
    ``_execute_processing`` as ``trusted_video_id`` — the wiring that makes win #2 fire on the drain."""
    db = Database(str(tmp_path / "jobs.db"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(m, "db_instance", db)
    monkeypatch.setattr(m, "global_config", AppConfig())
    seen = {}

    def _spy_exec(config, db_, req, progress=None, trusted_video_id=None):
        seen["trusted"] = trusted_video_id
        return {"status": "success", "video_id": _VALID_ID, "play_segments": []}

    monkeypatch.setattr(m, "_execute_processing", _spy_exec)
    db.create_job("pj", "gs://b/clip.mp4")
    db.set_job_video_id("pj", _VALID_ID)
    m._run_claimed_job(db.get_job("pj"), m.global_config, m.db_instance)
    assert seen["trusted"] == _VALID_ID


def test_run_claimed_job_passes_none_when_job_has_no_video_id(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """A job with no known video_id (local upload) threads None → the drain hashes (fallback)."""
    db = Database(str(tmp_path / "jobs.db"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(m, "db_instance", db)
    monkeypatch.setattr(m, "global_config", AppConfig())
    seen = {}

    def _spy_exec(config, db_, req, progress=None, trusted_video_id=None):
        seen["trusted"] = trusted_video_id
        return {"status": "success", "video_id": "x", "play_segments": []}

    monkeypatch.setattr(m, "_execute_processing", _spy_exec)
    db.create_job("pj2", "/local/v.mp4")  # no video_id set
    m._run_claimed_job(db.get_job("pj2"), m.global_config, m.db_instance)
    assert seen["trusted"] is None

### 177. assistant (2026-07-08T06:27:38.935Z)

## What & why

Epic **#524** (collapse the pipeline onto the L4 worker; shrink the control-plane to a thin router) is **investigation-first**. This PR lands the two lowest-risk, reversible pieces and defers the risky rearchitecture behind a reviewed design:

1. **Design doc** — `docs/COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING/L4_PRIMARY_TOPOLOGY.md` resolves the compute-topology question (**A** Cloud Run Service-with-GPU / **B** GCE L4 VM / **C** hybrid presigned-upload) with a cost model, and **recommends against the epic's proposed "upload directly onto the L4"**:
   - A same-region GCS↔Cloud Run transfer is **LAN-fast and not billed as internet egress** `[researched]`, so the round-trip that upload-onto-L4 saves is worth ~seconds and ~$0, while keeping an L4 up to *receive* a **minutes-long** upload burns **~175 s of idle GPU/run (~$0.08–0.16)** and *opens* a durability window instead of closing one.
   - The cheaper, more durable, already-ratified path is the opposite: **keep upload → bucket** (the #480 design; the durable copy exists before the L4 starts) and **collapse validate+detect+stitch onto the scale-to-zero Cloud Run L4 Job** via #521's `phase_placement`. The residual ~30 s gap dies not by moving the upload but by the control-plane **never downloading the file** (delegate validation per #521 + trust the md5 per win #2). Net movement: **3× → the floor (1 client upload + 1 LAN pull)**, zero idle-GPU cost, zero exposure window.
   - Persistent-GPU (VM / Service-with-min-instances) is **rejected at current volume** (breakeven ≈ 51 % on-demand / 31 % spot duty cycle ≈ 2.6–4.3k runs/mo) and left as an owner-gated future ADR. Honors ADR-014 **D4/D7/D16**.

2. **Win #2 — drop the redundant control-plane re-hash.** The drain re-hashed the whole (often multi-GB) clip it just fetched (`orchestration.py` `compute_video_id`), even though the submit hook already recorded the file's md5 on the job row (`submit_time_video_id`, #484 — a GCS-metadata md5 converted to the identical hex form; **already trusted for the submit-time cache probe**). Now the drain reuses that **server-derived, byte-identical** id and skips the re-hash; absent / malformed → it hashes exactly as before. Gated by `deployment.trust_submit_video_id` (default on) and guarded by `_looks_like_video_id` (strict 32-char lowercase hex).

**Win #1 (validation-on-L4) is intentionally NOT here** — it is delivered by the separate open branch `feat/521-compile-on-l4` (`phase_placement.validation=cloud_run_l4` + its guards). Per the coordination note in #524, this PR does **not** duplicate that knob; the design doc credits #521 and specifies the download-avoidance follow-up (which composes #521's delegation with this PR's win #2) plus the verdict-serialization gap #521 leaves open.

## Coordination with #521

- Branched from the **latest `origin/master`**; #521 is still open. Win #2 touches the top-of-`_execute_processing` hash block, which **#521 does not modify** → independent, non-conflicting.
- The new `deployment.trust_submit_video_id` field is a **non-overlapping addition** to `DeploymentConfig` (#521 adds `phase_placement`/`cloud_stitch_encoder` elsewhere in the class).

## Reversibility

Every change is off-by-config: `deployment.trust_submit_video_id=false` reverts win #2 to a byte-identical re-hash with no redeploy. When no trusted id is present (local/appliance upload, non-gcs direct-URI submit) the drain hashes as before. The resulting `video_id` is byte-identical to today's re-hash (same bytes, same MD5), so no cache/DB key changes.

## Verification (all local, gates match CI)

- `python run_all_tests.py --cov=backend --cov=training --cov-fail-under=70` → **1854 passed, 8 skipped, coverage 85.71%** (floor 70%).
- `ruff check .` clean · `mypy backend training` clean (149 files) · `python -m tools.leak_scan` clean · `python -m tools.check_md_links` clean.
- New `tests/test_video_id_trust.py` proves: trusted id → `compute_video_id` **not** called; absent/malformed/knob-off → re-hash; and the `_run_claimed_job` wiring threads `jobs.video_id` through.
- Not verifiable locally (owner-side, D17 budget): the live CUJ confirming the ~30 s gap is gone + movement is 2× on rev ≥ `00020`. Design + code + tests only — **no deploy** in this PR.

Refs #524, #521, #520.

6:> **Relation to existing docs:** extends [COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING/README.md](./README.md) (ADR-014) and [`PHASE_PLACEMENT.md` (#521)](https://github.com/Khelsutra/badminton-highlight-indexer/blob/feat/521-compile-on-l4/docs/COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING/PHASE_PLACEMENT.md); it does **not** supersede them — it answers the *topology* question they left open and sequences the migration on top of `phase_placement`.
13:The 2026-07-08 live CUJ moved one 1.83 GiB clip **3×** (upload→bucket, bucket→control-plane to validate, bucket→worker to detect) and pinned the CPU-only 2-vCPU control-plane for **~16 min** of heavy work (validation 183 s + stitch ~12m55s). The epic proposes fixing this by **uploading directly onto the L4** and async-copying to the bucket. **This design recommends *against* that direction** and quantifies why: a same-region bucket↔compute transfer inside GCP is **LAN-fast and not billed as internet egress** `[researched]`, so the round-trip that upload-onto-L4 saves is worth **~seconds and ~$0**, while keeping an L4 up to *receive* a **minutes-long** upload burns **~175 s of idle GPU per run (~$0.08–0.16)** `[design]` and *opens* a durability exposure window instead of closing one.
17:**Actionable now (this PR):** the design doc + **win #2** (drop the redundant control-plane re-hash, config-guarded, reversible). **Win #1** (validation-on-L4) is **delivered by #521** — this doc does not duplicate its knob. A persistent GPU (GCE VM / Cloud Run Service-with-GPU) is **rejected at current volume** on a quantified cost basis and left as an owner-gated future ADR.
23:1. **Data is moved 3× per run** `[verified]`: (a) upload streams straight to `gs://…/uploads/…` (never touches control-plane disk — the #480/#471 OOM fix, `backend/api/uploads.py`), computing the md5 incrementally as it streams; (b) the control-plane re-downloads the whole clip to scratch to validate — `_execute_processing` → `storage.acquire_local_input(gs://…)` at `backend/orchestration.py:748`, then a **redundant re-hash** `compute_video_id` at `:751`; (c) the worker downloads it a third time to detect (`[worker] pulled …`). The unlogged **~30 s pre-validation gap** is finding (b).
30:**Read to get current:** [`PHASE_PLACEMENT.md` (#521)](https://github.com/Khelsutra/badminton-highlight-indexer/blob/feat/521-compile-on-l4/docs/COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING/PHASE_PLACEMENT.md) — the `phase_placement` knob this sequences on, [README.md](./README.md) §2 decisions **D4/D7/D16** (the ratified constraints that bound the options), and the runlog §9 (the evidence).
144:| **Signed-URL download** (`/api/highlights` 307) | Reel is already in the bucket; CP just signs | `backend/main.py::get_highlights` |
165:| **P1** | **Stitch → L4** | `phase_placement.compile=cloud_run_l4` (+ `cloud_stitch_encoder`) | in-process stitch | **#521 (open)** |
167:| **P2b** | **Validation → L4** (CP stops running validators) | `phase_placement.validation=cloud_run_l4` (guards: requires cloud segment) | CP validates | **#521 (open)** |
168:| **P2 net** | **CP never downloads the file** → the ~30 s gap dies; movement 3×→2× | P2a ∧ P2b together | either half alone degrades gracefully | needs #521 **+** win #2 merged |
191:| P2 | **Win #1** — validation-on-L4 (`phase_placement.validation`) | #521 | No | ☑ **delivered by #521** — not duplicated here | #521 |

198	**Blocking (owner sign-off):**
199	1. **Do you accept rejecting "upload directly onto the L4" (L5)?** The epic proposed it; this doc recommends against it with the §4.2/§4.3 idle-GPU-vs-free-LAN-pull numbers. A′ delivers the same "collapse onto the L4" outcome, cheaper and more durable, via config. *If you still want upload-onto-L4* (e.g. for a future no-bucket edge deployment), §4.5 specs its durability model — but it needs a Service-with-GPU or VM (a D16(a)/D7 reversal → new ADR).
200	2. **Flip P2b (validation→L4) before or after P3 (verdict serialization)?** P2b alone offloads CPU but degrades the reject-UX + skips #518 cache-warm (§6). Recommend: land P3 first, then P2b.

172	**The "collapse onto the L4" the epic asks for = P1 + P2, both config flips on #521's knob + this PR's win #2.** No rearchitecture, no new infra, fully reversible.
173	
174	## 6. Honest limits — what this does NOT do / does NOT prove
175	
176	- **A green suite does not prove the cloud path** — the tests are fully-mocked/offline (a fake `CloudRunJobClient`). P2's "CP never downloads" and the idle-GPU/round-trip numbers are validated **in the cost model + code paths**, not on live infra. A live CUJ on rev ≥ `00020` (owner-side, D17 budget) is required to *confirm* the gap is gone and the movement is 2×.
177	- **Network-egress "free intra-region" is `[researched]`, not live-verified** — general web egress is blocked here, so same-region GCS↔Cloud Run being un-billed-as-internet-egress rests on documented GCP behavior, not a fetched invoice. If it were *not* free, A′ is *still* cheapest (it moves the fewest bytes); the conclusion is robust to this uncertainty.
178	- **Win #2 changes nothing when no trusted id is present** — local/appliance uploads and non-GCS direct-URI submits (`submit_time_video_id` → `None`) still re-hash. The win applies to the GCS cloud-serving path (the CUJ case).
179	- **P3 (worker validation-verdict serialization) is a real gap #521 leaves open** `[verified]`: today a worker-side validation reject surfaces as `rc≠0` + a message, but the per-validator `ValidationResult` list (`result['results']` the SPA renders) and the #518 `validation_cache` warm are **not** reconstructed on the CP. Until P3, moving validation to the L4 (P2b) trades richer verdict UX + cache-warming for the CPU offload. Called out so P2b isn't flipped on blind.
180	- **SQLite is the residual CP scaling limit** — the thin router is stateless *except* the SQLite DB; true horizontal scale-out needs Postgres (out of scope; noted).
181	- **The #513 asymmetry reverses under P2b** `[verified]`: worker-side validators run at model-default thresholds unless the CP forwards `validation.*` as `--set`. #521's validation-delegation path must forward those (or re-inherit the divergence that caused the #513 incident). Flagged for the #521 review, not owned here.

### 277. assistant (2026-07-08T07:02:46.012Z)

## What & why

Epic **#524** (collapse the pipeline onto the L4 worker; shrink the control-plane to a thin router) is **investigation-first**. This PR lands the two lowest-risk, reversible pieces and defers the risky rearchitecture behind a reviewed design.

### 1. Design doc — `docs/COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING/L4_PRIMARY_TOPOLOGY.md`

Resolves the compute-topology question (**A** Cloud Run Service-with-GPU / **B** GCE L4 VM / **C** hybrid presigned-upload) with a cost model, and **recommends against the epic's proposed "upload directly onto the L4"**:

- A same-region GCS↔Cloud Run transfer is **LAN-fast and not billed as internet egress** `[researched]`, so the round-trip that upload-onto-L4 saves is worth ~seconds and ~$0, while keeping an L4 up to *receive* a **minutes-long** upload burns **~175 s of idle GPU/run (~$0.08–0.13)** and *opens* a durability window instead of closing one.
- The cheaper, more durable, already-ratified path is the opposite: **keep upload → bucket** (the #480 design; the durable copy exists before the L4 starts) and **collapse validate+detect+stitch onto the scale-to-zero Cloud Run L4 Job** via #521's `phase_placement`. The residual ~30 s gap dies not by moving the upload but by the control-plane **never downloading the file** (delegate validation per #521 + trust the md5 per win #2). Net movement: **3× → the floor (1 client upload + 1 LAN pull)**, zero idle-GPU cost, zero exposure window.
- Persistent-GPU (VM / Service-with-min-instances) is **rejected at current volume** (breakeven ≈ 51 % on-demand / 31 % spot duty cycle ≈ 2.6–4.3k runs/mo) and left as an owner-gated future ADR. Honors ADR-014 **D4/D7/D16**.

The doc also specifies the minimal control-plane surface, the async copy-to-bucket durability model (failure/resume + exposure window), and a phased migration where each move is a config flip with a bucket-mediated fallback.

### 2. Win #2 — drop the redundant control-plane re-hash

The drain re-hashed the whole (often multi-GB) clip it just fetched, even though the submit hook already recorded the file's md5 on the job row (`submit_time_video_id`, #484 — the GCS-metadata md5 in hex, **already trusted for the submit-time cache probe**). Now the drain reuses that **server-derived, byte-identical** id and skips the re-hash; absent / malformed → it hashes exactly as before. Gated by `deployment.trust_submit_video_id` (default on) and guarded by `_looks_like_video_id` (strict 32-char lowercase hex).

**Win #1 (validation-on-L4) is intentionally NOT here** — it is delivered by **#521, merged to master as #525** (`phase_placement.validation=cloud_run_l4` + its guards). Per the coordination note in #524, this PR does **not** duplicate that knob; the design doc credits #521 and specifies the download-avoidance follow-up (which composes #521's delegation with this PR's win #2) plus the verdict-serialization gap #521 leaves open.

## Coordination / rebase safety

- Rebased onto the **latest `origin/master`**, which now includes #521 (#525) **and** its #527/#528 follow-ups. The diff is **exactly 7 files** — no sibling work reverted (verified `git diff --stat origin/master`).
- `deployment.trust_submit_video_id` is a **non-overlapping addition** to `DeploymentConfig` alongside #521's `phase_placement`/`cloud_stitch_encoder`.

## Reversibility

Off-by-config: `deployment.trust_submit_video_id=false` reverts win #2 to a byte-identical re-hash, no redeploy. When no trusted id is present (local/appliance upload, non-gcs direct-URI submit) the drain hashes as before. The resulting `video_id` is byte-identical (same bytes, same MD5), so no cache/DB key changes.

## Verification (all local, gates match CI)

- `python run_all_tests.py --cov=backend --cov=training --cov-fail-under=70` → **1889 passed, 8 skipped, coverage 85.83%** (floor 70%).
- `ruff check .` clean · `mypy backend training` clean (150 files) · `python -m tools.leak_scan` clean · `python -m tools.check_md_links` clean.
- New `tests/test_video_id_trust.py` proves: trusted id → `compute_video_id` **not** called; absent/malformed/knob-off → re-hash; and the `_run_claimed_job` wiring threads `jobs.video_id` through.
- **Not verifiable locally** (owner-side, D17 budget): the live CUJ confirming the ~30 s gap is gone + movement is 2× on rev ≥ `00020`. Design + code + tests only — **no deploy** in this PR.

## Review

An adversarial 4-dimension multi-agent review (correctness · security/trust-boundary · doc-accuracy · conventions), each finding independently verified, was folded in: fixed a doc anchor naming a nonexistent symbol, reconciled statuses now that #521 merged, narrowed an unsupported cost bound, and added an honest §6 caveat that win #2's byte-identity rests on in-flight object immutability (safe in today's server-mediated-upload topology; a future presigned/client-writable upload path must generation-pin before enabling the trust). Correctness dimension was clean; the trust-boundary TOCTOU was verified **unreachable** in the current topology (documented, not coded, correctly scoped out).

Refs #524, #521, #520.

## Summary

**PR [#530](https://github.com/Khelsutra/badminton-highlight-indexer/pull/530)** — `feat/524-l4-primary` → `master`, **all CI green.**

### What shipped (2 of 3 deliverables; #3 was correctly a "don't ship yet")

**1. Design doc** — [`docs/COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING/L4_PRIMARY_TOPOLOGY.md`](docs/COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING/L4_PRIMARY_TOPOLOGY.md). Resolves the A/B/C topology question with a cost model pulled live from the GCP Billing Catalog API. **Recommends *against* the epic's "upload onto the L4"**: the central quantified finding is that uploading onto the GPU box trades a **~free, seconds-fast intra-region LAN pull for ~175 s of idle GPU per run**, and *opens* a durability window. The cheaper/more-durable path — co-located scale-to-zero Jobs + bucket upload — kills the ~30 s gap by the control-plane **never downloading the file** (composing #521's validation-delegation with win #2). Persistent-GPU rejected on a breakeven analysis. Includes the minimal control-plane surface, async-copy durability spec, and a config-flip phased migration.

**2. Win #2 (re-hash)** — the drain now trusts the **server-derived** submit-time md5 (`submit_time_video_id`, #484 — already trusted for the cache probe) instead of re-hashing the fetched clip. Gated by `deployment.trust_submit_video_id` (default on), strict hex-format guard, byte-identical id, fully reversible. New `tests/test_video_id_trust.py` proves trust-vs-rehash + the drain wiring.

**Win #1 (validation-on-L4)** — **not duplicated**; delivered by #521 (merged as #525). The doc credits it and specifies the follow-ups (#521 leaves the worker verdict-serialization gap open).

### Rigor applied
- 8-agent subsystem map + adversarial 4-dimension review (each finding independently verified). Fixed a doc anchor naming a nonexistent symbol, narrowed an unsupported cost bound, added an honest TOCTOU/immutability caveat. Correctness dimension was clean.
- **All gates green locally and on CI**: full suite 1889 passed / coverage 85.83% (floor 70%), ruff, mypy, leak_scan, check_md_links.

### One hazard caught and corrected
`origin/master` advanced twice mid-session (#521 → #527/#528). My first commit would have **reverted #527/#528** (the exact "sibling-work absorbed" incident the guardrails warn about). I detected it via `git diff --stat origin/master`, restored the 6 sibling files to master, re-merged only my win#2 edits into the current `orchestration.py`, and reverified — the final diff is **exactly my 7 files, no sibling reverts**.

### Two things left for you
- **§8 blocking question:** do you accept rejecting "upload directly onto the L4" (the doc's L5 decision)? My recommendation is against it, with the numbers — but it's your call since it contradicts the epic's proposed direction.
- **Owner-side (D17 budget):** a live CUJ on rev ≥ `00020` to confirm the ~30 s gap is gone and movement is 2×. No deploy was in scope here.

I did **not** touch the main checkout's `SESSION_HANDOFF.md` — it's on the `codex/issue-523` branch with another session's WIP, and the concurrent-session guardrail says not to clobber it.

### 286. user (2026-07-08T07:07:01.082Z)

is the win in this PR enabled by default?

### 287. assistant (2026-07-08T07:07:27.636Z)

Yes — **on by default**, with one practical qualifier.

**The knob:** `deployment.trust_submit_video_id` defaults to `True`. So the trust path is active out of the box (no config change needed), and it's a one-line rollback (`false`) if it ever needs disabling.

**Where it actually fires:** the win only takes effect when a trusted `video_id` is present on the job row, which requires the input to be a **GCS object** — `submit_time_video_id` gets it from one gs:// metadata stat (#484). For **local/appliance uploads and non-GCS direct-URI submits** it returns `None`, so the drain re-hashes exactly as before. In other words:

| Path | Default behavior with this PR |
|---|---|
| GCS cloud-serving upload (the CUJ case) | **skips the re-hash** — the win |
| Local / appliance upload | re-hashes (unchanged) |
| Non-GCS direct URI (s3/gdrive) | re-hashes (unchanged) |
| Knob set to `false` | re-hashes everywhere (unchanged) |

**Why it's safe to default on** (all verified, and the adversarial review's correctness dimension was clean):
- The trusted id is **server-derived**, not client-supplied — a caller's `/api/process` body can't inject it (the submit hook recomputes the authoritative id).
- The result is **byte-identical** to today's re-hash (same bytes, same MD5), so no cache key, DB key, or output path changes.
- It's the **same value the submit-time cache probe already trusts** to complete jobs with zero re-hash — this PR just extends that established trust to the cache-miss drain path.
- A strict 32-char-lowercase-hex guard means any malformed/absent id silently falls back to a real hash.

The one honest caveat I flagged in the doc (§6): the byte-identity rests on the bucket object being immutable in-flight, which holds in today's server-mediated-upload topology. If a future presigned/client-writable upload path is ever added, it must generation-pin before relying on this trust — but that's not reachable today.

Resolved — and it was **not** my doing. The reflog shows the concurrent **#523 session was mid-rebase** onto the updated `origin/master` (the one that now includes my #524) exactly when I ran `git status`. A rebase detaches HEAD and stages its picked files transiently; between that call and the next, the rebase **finished** and HEAD re-attached:

```
3598082 HEAD@{0}: rebase (finish): returning to refs/heads/codex/issue-523-sd-reel-plan
304af7b HEAD@{2}: rebase (start): checkout origin/master   ← rebasing onto master-with-#524
```

`git branch` now confirms the checkout is cleanly back on `* codex/issue-523-sd-reel-plan`. This is precisely the "git state changes under you between two commands" race the guardrails warn about — I correctly left the shared checkout untouched.
